		private void Post_Generate()
		{
			MmgExtensions.GxDriveDialog2((MMViewModel)(object)this, "ProgressBar", new object[2] { 1, 3 });
			((MMViewModel)this).CloseForm();
			((MMViewModel)this).UpdateAll();
		}

		private static double SigFigs(double value, int places)
		{
			int num = 0;
			if (value == 0.0 || value == 1E+35)
			{
				return 0.0;
			}
			num = (int)(Math.Log(Math.Abs(value)) / Math.Log(10.0));
			return Round(value * Math.Pow(10.0, -num), -(places - 1)) * Math.Pow(10.0, num);
		}

		private static double Round(double value, int places)
		{
			return Convert.ToDouble(Convert.ToInt64(value * Math.Pow(10.0, -places))) / Math.Pow(10.0, -places);
		}

		private void ClearFracData()
		{
			string text = "@main.Input.X1_EST";
			string text2 = "@main.Input.X2_EST";
			string[] array = CxsConverter.ValueToStringArray(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet(text, "child_ids"));
			if (array != null && array.Length != 0)
			{
				((MMViewModel)this).DeleteChildren(text, (IEnumerable<string>)array, true);
			}
			string[] array2 = CxsConverter.ValueToStringArray(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet(text2, "child_ids"));
			if (array2 != null && array2.Length != 0)
			{
				((MMViewModel)this).DeleteChildren(text2, (IEnumerable<string>)array2, true);
			}
		}

		private void cmdClose_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			((MMViewModel)this).CloseForm();
		}
